Buɗe baki t ayi saidai , bat akai ga maganar ba t a t saida bakint a a buɗe saboda bugun ƙofarsu da Akeyi, cikin kuka wat a mace t ake ƙwalla masa kira ya fit o haj iya ba lafiya

Wani hararan wasa t a banka masa "uhmuhmnaƙi wayon , Ance maka ni ƴal iska ce"

Haj iya suwaiba yamma na ƙara yi t a shirya zuwa mot her's eve ɗin ƴar ƙawart a a Hanwa , saidai yai saida t a zage driver  t at ass t a sashi yayi wanka t a bashi sabbin kaya wai ƙazant arshi ya ishet a kowa drivern shi t saf amma banda it a
Shikansa saida yayi mamakin kansa yanda ya ɗaura yadin a j ikinsa ya karɓesa ya zauna a bakin ƙofar falon yina j ir an fit owart a
Cikin wani  Maroon     ɗin  Mat erial  l ace mai t sadar  gaske, pat ch designers wanda ya  kasance ashoben bikin, t a kafa ɗaurin gwagwaro sai zuba uban ƙamshi t ake t a fit o hannunt a riƙe da CG bag
"Ehemmm ko kaifa amma da kana mun yawo t summa t summa"  miƙewa yayi yina faɗin "Haj iyar Allah " ya miƙa hannu zai karɓi j akan hannunt a kamar yanda ya saba in zasu fit a

"Ahh No bar mun inaj in Nishaɗin in riƙe" t a t aka t inƙis t inƙis kamar mej in Tausayin ƙasa t aj e t a Shiga gaban  red car  ɗint a , gefen  me zaman  banza  .  driver  kam wani soko ya zama duk t a birkit a masa lissafi t afe t ake amma fankat a²n ɗuwawunt a yike kallo ya buɗe ya shiga sukaj a suka t afi  amma  shi  mai yasa yai  haj iya t a  shiga  gaba  a  maimakon  baya ?  Me yasa t a  riƙe j akart a ?  Me yasa t a sashi yayi wanka t a bashi sabon kaya?  Tunanin da yikeyi kenan har ya harba babban t it i , kafin ya waigo ya kallet a

kallonsa .
"Haj iya lafiya kuwa?" ya magant u idonsa yina kan rigarsa mai ɗinkin kwaɓaɓɓen kafaɗu , Tafka t afkan nonuwant a kusan rabi suna waj e , ƙyar yayi masu da ido kamar  na second biyu kafin ya maza ya j anye ya cigaba da kallon hanya "Haj iya ina mukayi?"
"Hanwa" t a amsa masa a t aƙaice kafin t a gyara zamant a sosai , t a mat si waj en sit iyarin sosai har t ana j iyo saukar numfashinsa wanda ke ɗauke da Alamun t soro .

Cigaba  da  l ura  dashi t ayi  har  saida  suka  kai  cikin  goslow ɗin  pz  kafin  a  hankali t a  ɗakko
hannunt a t a sauke akan girmansa , zaro ido yayi ya kallet a kawai t a kashe masa ido
"Ha. . . ha. . . haj iya " murza Buransa t ayi da kyau
"I nason ka I di , ka bani buranka , zan sha buranka inason inj i garɗin madaran bindigarka acikin bakina , inaso inj i yawan madaranka a cikin gindina. . . . ka bani Kula in baka nishaɗi , ka bani bura in baka burin rayuwa. . . ma'ana ina nufin in baka shegun banki. . . !"
Kafin yayi  wani  magana  sukaj i  mot arsu  kamar  wanda  aka  ɗaga  akayi  wancakali  dashi  gefe aikuwa t a kama da wut a nan t ake mut ane suka shiga ihun kawo ɗauke , ana kallo mot a na ci da wut a an kasa Taimako. . . . .

R.A saidai  kafin suyi yunƙurin  guduwa  mot ocin  soj oj i  da  aka yaye sun xo crossing  aikuwa sukace dawa Allah ya haɗa, aka  fara t sere , nan  mut ane suka  fara  sauke  ababen  hawansu  daga  kwalt a  ana  basu hanya...su kuma su I di da aka lula duniyar  bura da cin duri basu j i ko ƙarar  mot ocin ba , har  saida mot ar ƴan fashin t a hamɓaresu cikin rami shine kifewar da mot ar t ayi , t a kama da wut a
